# Requirement traceability matrix - S_ecomm_comm_merch-supply-chain-vb_20260916_073356

Generated by `coverage_map.py --matrix`. One row per CORE ASK, one
column per grader, `YES` where that grader covers that ask. Rows,
columns and cells are the same data G24 decides on, so this matrix
cannot disagree with the gate or with the table.

`Subs` is how many sub-requirements the ask folds in; a bracketed
number is how many of them are ungraded. An ask is Covered only when
every one of them is graded.

Core asks: **247**  ·  graders: **210**  ·  not fully graded: **0**
